**Official motives for withdrawal**

The countries officially stated that they withdrew because **part of the conference program did not align with their governments' positions and national interests**:

- **The Lithuanian Ministry of Foreign Affairs** stated that it withdrew when it became clear that some parts of the program did not align with the Government's positions and Lithuania's national interests. The ministry emphasized that it did not participate in drafting the program.

- **Lithuanian Minister of Foreign Affairs Kęstutis Budrys** told LRT radio that he was "surprised and shocked" that the US was equated with Russia or China. He stated that the organizers "went way overboard and made a huge blunder", and that such rhetoric "weakens our ties with allies".

- **Canada** (Global Affairs Canada) stated that it "reviewed its participation" due to "altered wording of certain discussions".

- **The European External Action Service** asked to remove its logo "in light of the program's content."

**Consequences**

- The logos of the Canadian, Lithuanian, and EU diplomatic services were **covered up** on the conference stands

- These institutions were removed from the official program and the list of sponsors

- **France remained the sole major sponsor**

When asked to comment, the US State Department stated: *"While we cannot comment on the details of diplomatic conversations, it should not come as a surprise that countries are distancing themselves from this conference upon learning that it identifies the US as a 'FIMI threat'."*

**Original text**

ES, Kanada ir Lietuva atsiėmė paramą konferencijai **#Disinfo2026** (kuri vyko spalio 7–8 d. Vilniuje) dėl šių priežasčių:

**Pagrindinė priežastis: JAV apibūdinimas kaip dezinformacijos grėsmė**

Konferencijos, kurią organizavo Briuselyje įsikūrusi nevyriausybinė organizacija **EU DisinfoLab**, programoje buvo numatytos diskusijos, kuriose **JAV buvo įvardijamos kaip „užsienio vykdomo manipuliavimo informacija ir kišimosi" (FIMI – *Foreign Information Manipulation and Interference*) veikėjas**.

Viena iš sesijų buvo pavadinta **„JAV kaip FIMI grėsmė: pripažinti, prisitaikyti, atremti"** („USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back"). Joje du amerikiečių tyrėjai ir buvęs CŽA pareigūnas nagrinėjo, kaip JAV tapo dezinformacijos grėsme Europai.

**JAV administracijos spaudimas**

Pasak „Politico" ir „Reuters", **Donaldo Trumpo administracija darė spaudimą** Lietuvos, Kanados ir ES diplomatams, reikalaudama pasitraukti iš konferencijos:

- JAV Valstybės departamentas nurodė savo ambasadoms keliose Europos šalyse ir ES išreikšti Vašingtono susirūpinimą

- Buvo prašoma įvertinti, ar konferencijoje dalyvaujančios vyriausybės pritaria tam, kad JAV būtų priskiriamos prie FIMI veikėjų

- Dalyvavo JAV ambasadorė Lietuvoje **Kara McDonald**

- Po pokalbių su JAV pareigūnais Kanada ir Lietuva pirmadienį atšaukė paramą, antradienį – ES išorės veiksmų tarnyba (EIVT)

**Oficialūs pasitraukimo motyvai**

Šalys oficialiai teigė pasitraukusios dėl to, kad **dalis konferencijos programos neatitiko jų vyriausybių pozicijų ir nacionalinių interesų**:

- **Lietuvos URM** pareiškė, kad pasitraukė, kai paaiškėjo, jog kai kurios programos dalys neatitinka Vyriausybės pozicijų ir Lietuvos nacionalinių interesų. Ministerija pabrėžė nedalyvavusi rengiant programą.

- **Lietuvos užsienio reikalų ministras Kęstutis Budrys** LRT radijui sakė buvęs „nustebintas ir šokiruotas", kad JAV buvo prilyginta Rusijai ar Kinijai. Jis teigė, kad organizatoriai „peržengė visas ribas ir padarė didžiulę klaidą" (*„went way overboard and made a huge blunder"*), o tokia retorika „susilpnina mūsų ryšius su sąjungininkais".

- **Kanada** (Global Affairs Canada) pareiškė, kad „peržiūrėjo savo dalyvavimą" dėl „pakeisto kai kurių diskusijų formulavimo".

- **ES išorės veiksmų tarnyba** paprašė pašalinti jos logotipą „atsižvelgiant į programos turinį".

**Pasekmės**

- Kanados, Lietuvos ir ES diplomatinių tarnybų logotipai buvo **uždengti** konferencijos stenduose

- Šios institucijos buvo pašalintos iš oficialios programos ir rėmėjų sąrašo

- **Prancūzija liko vienintele pagrindine rėmėja**

JAV Valstybės departamentas, paklaustas pakomentuoti, pareiškė: *„Nors negalime komentuoti diplomatinių pokalbių detalių, neturėtų stebinti, kad šalys atsiriboja nuo šios konferencijos, sužinojusios, jog joje JAV įvardijama kaip „FIMI grėsmė".*

**English translation**

The EU, Canada, and Lithuania withdrew their support for the annual **#Disinfo2026** conference (organized by the NGO EU DisinfoLab), which took place on October 7–8, 2026, in Vilnius, due to several interrelated reasons:

**1. The Conference Program "Accusing" the US**

The main reason was that the program featured panels framing the US as a source of disinformation and an actor of "foreign information manipulation and interference" (FIMI — the EU term for hostile information operations):

- **"USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back"** — a panel featuring two American researchers and a former CIA officer, discussing "how the US became a disinformation threat to Europe."

- **"Under attack: How the counter-disinformation community is dealing with US government pressure"** — a panel about US government pressure, including visa bans for European experts.

- A panel on the Arctic, which claimed there is "growing evidence" that the US acted as a FIMI actor in the context of Greenland.

**2. Pressure from the Trump Administration**

According to reports by **Politico**, **Reuters**, and **The Guardian**, the US State Department sent diplomatic cables to US embassies in European countries, Canada, and Lithuania demanding to: - express Washington's concerns; - "assess" whether participating governments agree with characterizing the US as a FIMI actor; - reconsider participation in the conference.

US Ambassador to Lithuania **Kara McDonald** personally participated in negotiations with the Lithuanian side. Internal State Department documents showed that the US was "grateful" to Lithuania for "taking American concerns seriously."

**3. Media and Social Media Campaign**

The conference came under attack from **MAGA bloggers** and conservative activists. In particular, journalist **Michael Shellenberger** sent "intimidating emails" to participants questioning their involvement, funding, and stance on the US as a source of FIMI. This created additional pressure on the organizers and sponsors.

**4. Official Statements from Those Who Withdrew Support**

All three parties cited the fact that the program "did not align with the official position":

**Lithuania**: The Ministry of Foreign Affairs stated that "parts of the program do not correspond to the official position and national interests of Lithuania." Foreign Minister **Kęstutis Budrys** said in an interview with LRT Radio that the organizers "went too far and made a huge mistake" and that he was "surprised and shocked" that the US was put on the same level as Russia or China: *“This does not reflect our views and principles... the fight against disinformation should not turn into an anti-American narrative that weakens our ties with allies.”*

**Canada** (Global Affairs Canada): stated that it "reconsidered its participation" after "changes in the phrasing of several panels." Spokesperson Samantha Lafleur confirmed that the government was "uncomfortable" with the wording of certain panels. At the same time, Canadian officials continued to participate in the conference, but dropped out of chairing the Arctic session.

**The EU** (European External Action Service, EEAS): stated that it asked to have its logo removed because the organizers "framed some discussions in a way that does not align with the EU's official positions." The EU did not agree with the characterization of the US as a disinformation actor, though it continued funding the conference.

**Conclusion**

At the conference, the logos of Canada, Lithuania, and ЕС were **covered with white stickers** on banners and materials. The only remaining major state sponsor was the **French Ministry of Foreign Affairs**, which reaffirmed its support for the conference and the role of civil society in countering disinformation.

Thus, the withdrawal of support was the result of **a combination of direct US diplomatic pressure** and the **inconsistency between the conference content and the official positions** of the three governments, which were not ready to be associated with a narrative equating the US with hostile disinformation actors like Russia or China.

**Original text**

ЕС, Канада и Литва отозвали поддержку ежегодной конференции **#Disinfo2026** (организатор — НКО EU DisinfoLab), проходившей 7–8 октября 2026 года в Вильнюсе, по нескольким взаимосвязанным причинам:

**1. Программа конференции, «обвиняющая» США**

Главная причина — в программе были заявлены панели, в которых США рассматривались как источник дезинформации и актор «иностранного информационного манипулирования и вмешательства» (FIMI — термин ЕС для обозначения враждебных информационных операций):

- **«USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back»** («США как угроза FIMI: признать, адаптироваться, дать отпор») — панель с участием двух американских исследователей и бывшего сотрудника ЦРУ, где обсуждалось, «как США стали угрозой дезинформации для Европы».

- **«Under attack: How the counter-disinformation community is dealing with US government pressure»** — панель о давлении правительства США, включая визовые запреты для европейских экспертов.

- Панель об Арктике, где утверждалось о «растущих доказательствах» того, что США действовали как FIMI-актор в контексте Гренландии.

**2. Давление администрации Трампа**

Согласно материалам **Politico**, **Reuters** и **The Guardian**, Госдепартамент США разослал дипломатические телеграммы американским посольствам в европейских странах, Канаде и Литве с требованием: - выразить обеспокоенность Вашингтона; - «оценить», согласны ли правительства-участники с характеристикой США как FIMI-актора; - пересмотреть участие в конференции.

Посол США в Литве **Кара Макдональд** лично участвовала в переговорах с литовской стороной. Внутренние документы Госдепа показывали, что США «благодарны» Литве за то, что она «серьёзно отнеслась к американским обеспокоенностям».

**3. Кампания в СМИ и соцсетях**

Конференция подверглась атакам со стороны **MAGA-блогеров** и консервативных активистов. В частности, журналист **Майкл Шелленбергер** (Michael Shellenberger) рассылал участникам «запугивающие письма» с расспросами об их участии, финансировании и позиции по поводу США как источника FIMI. Это создало дополнительное давление на организаторов и спонсоров.

**4. Официальные заявления отозвавших поддержку**

Все три стороны сослались на то, что программа «не соответствует официальной позиции»:

**Литва**: МИД заявил, что «части программы не соответствуют официальной позиции правительства и национальным интересам Литвы». Глава МИД **Кестутис Будрис** в интервью LRT Radio сказал, что организаторы «перестарались и совершили огромную ошибку» и что он «удивлён и шокирован» тем, что США поставили в один ряд с Россией или Китаем: *«Это не соответствует нашим взглядам и принципам... тема борьбы с дезинформацией не должна превращаться в антиамериканский нарратив, ослабляющий наши связи с союзниками»*.

**Канада** (Global Affairs Canada): заявила, что «пересмотрела своё участие» после «изменений в формулировках нескольких панелей». Представительница Саманта Лафлёр подтвердила, что правительству «неудобна» формулировка некоторых панелей. При этом канадские чиновники продолжили участие в конференции, но отказались от кресла председателя сессии по Арктике.

**ЕС** (Европейская служба внешних действий, EEAS): заявила, что попросила убрать свой логотип, поскольку организаторы «сформулировали некоторые дискуссии так, что это не соответствует официальным позициям ЕС». ЕС не согласился с характеристикой США как дезинформационного актора, хотя продолжил финансирование конференции.

**Итог**

На конференции логотипы Канады, Литвы и ЕС были **заклеены белыми стicker’ами** на баннерах и материалах. Единственным крупным государственным спонсором осталось **Министерство иностранных дел Франции**, которое подтвердило поддержку конференции и роли гражданского общества в борьбе с дезинформацией.

Таким образом, отзыв поддержки стал результатом **сочетания прямого дипломатического давления США** и **несоответствия содержания конференции официальным позициям** трёх правительств, которые не готовы были ассоциироваться с нарративом, приравнивающим США к враждебным дезинформационным акторам вроде России или Китая.

**English translation**

The EU, Canada, and Lithuania withdrew their support for the **#Disinfo2026** conference (organized by the Brussels-based NGO **EU DisinfoLab**), which took place on October 7–8, 2026, in Vilnius, due to several interrelated reasons:

**1. The conference program put the US on par with hostile regimes**

The main reason was that the program featured panels that directly treated **the US as a source of "Foreign Information Manipulation and Interference" (FIMI)**. Specifically, one session was titled:

> **"USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back"**

The program also included a panel titled **"Under attack: How the counter-disinformation community is dealing with US government pressure"**, where lawyers were to assess the threat of US entry bans for European figures.

**2. Pressure from the Trump administration**

According to reports by **Politico**, **Reuters**, and **The Guardian**, the US State Department sent a diplomatic cable instructing US embassies in Europe, Canada, and at the EU to express Washington's concern and "assess" whether governments agreed with the characterization of the US as a FIMI actor.

US diplomats (including US Ambassador to Lithuania **Kara McDonald**) spent several days "working over" the governments of Lithuania, Canada, and France. As a result, Canada and Lithuania asked the organizers to remove their logos from conference materials on Monday, and the European External Action Service (EEAS) did so on Tuesday.

**3. Official statements from the countries**

All three parties cited the fact that the program **did not align with their official position**:

**Lithuania**: The Ministry of Foreign Affairs stated that it "canceled its participation because several parts of the program differ from the official government position." Minister of Foreign Affairs **Kęstutis Budrys**, in an interview with LRT Radio, called the organizers' actions "going too far and a big mistake," stating that he was "surprised and shocked" that the US was put on the same level as Russia or China. According to him, the fight against disinformation must not turn into **"an anti-American narrative that weakens ties with allies."**

**Canada**: Global Affairs Canada spokesperson **Samantha Lafleur** stated that the government was "not comfortable with the wording of some panels," and Canada had "reconsidered its participation" in these sessions. At the same time, Canadian officials continued to participate in the conference, but without sponsor status and without chairing discussions.

**EU (EEAS)**: A spokesperson stated that the service asked to remove its logo **"due to the content of the program,"** as the organizers **"chose the wording of some discussions in a way that does not correspond to the official positions of the EU."**

**4. What happened in practice**

- The logos of the Lithuanian Ministry of Foreign Affairs, Global Affairs Canada, and the EEAS were **covered with white stickers** on stands and conference materials.

- **France** remained the only major sponsor.

- Despite the withdrawal of support, **the EU continues to fund** the conference, and Canada maintains the participation of its representatives (though not as organizers).

**Summary**

The countries withdrew their support not because of the topic of countering disinformation itself, but because of the **specific wording of the program**, which, in their view, unreasonably equated the US with hostile states (Russia, China) in the sphere of information influence. This occurred under direct diplomatic pressure from the Trump administration, for which such a characterization was unacceptable.

**Original text**

ЕС, Канада и Литва отозвали поддержку конференции **#Disinfo2026** (организованной Brussels-based НКО **EU DisinfoLab**), которая проходила 7–8 октября 2026 года в Вильнюсе, по нескольким взаимосвязанным причинам:

**1. Программа конференции ставила США в один ряд с враждебными режимами**

Главная причина — в программе были заявлены панели, которые прямо рассматривали **США как источник «иностранного информационного манипулирования и вмешательства» (FIMI)**. В частности, сессия называлась:

> **«USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back»** («США как угроза FIMI: признать, адаптироваться, дать отпор»)

В программе также значилась панель **«Under attack: How the counter-disinformation community is dealing with US government pressure»** («Под атакой: как сообщество по борьбе с дезинформацией справляется с давлением правительства США»), где юристы должны были оценить угрозу запретов на въезд в США для европейских деятелей.

**2. Давление администрации Трампа**

Согласно материалам **Politico**, **Reuters** и **The Guardian**, Госдепартамент США направил дипломатическую депешу, в которой американские посольства в Европе, Канаде и при ЕС должны были выразить обеспокоенность Вашингтона и «оценить», согласны ли правительства с характеристикой США как FIMI-актора.

Американские дипломаты (включая посла США в Литве **Кару Макдональд**) несколько дней «обрабатывали» правительства Литвы, Канады и Франции. В результате Канада и Литва попросили организаторов убрать их логотипы с материалов конференции в понедельник, а Европейская служба внешних связей (EEAS) — во вторник.

**3. Официальные заявления стран**

Все три стороны сослались на то, что программа **не соответствует их официальной позиции**:

**Литва**: МИД заявил, что «отменил участие, поскольку несколько частей программы отличаются от официальной позиции правительства». Министр иностранных дел **Кестутис Будрис** в интервью LRT Radio назвал действия организаторов «перебором и большой ошибкой», заявив, что он «удивлён и шокирован» тем, что США поставлены в один ряд с Россией или Китаем. По его словам, борьба с дезинформацией не должна превращаться в **«антиамериканский нарратив, ослабляющий связи с союзниками»**.

**Канада**: Представитель Global Affairs Canada **Саманта Лафлер** заявила, что правительство «не устраивает формулировка некоторых панелей», и Kanada «пересмотрела своё участие» в этих сессиях. При этом канадские чиновники продолжили участвовать в конференции, но без статуса спонсора и без председательства в дискуссиях.

**ЕС (EEAS)**: Пресс-секретарь заявил, что служба попросила убрать её логотип «в связи с содержанием программы», поскольку организаторы «выбрали формулировку некоторых дискуссий так, что она не соответствует официальным позициям ЕС».

**4. Что произошло на практике**

- Логотипы МИД Литвы, Global Affairs Canada и EEAS были **заклеены белыми стикерами** на стендах и материалах конференции.

- **Франция** осталась единственным крупным спонсором.

- Несмотря на отзыв поддержки, **ЕС продолжает финансировать** конференцию, а Канада сохраняет участие своих представителей (но не в качестве организаторов).

**Итог**

Страны отозвали поддержку не из-за самой темы борьбы с дезинформацией, а из-за **конкретной формулировки программы**, которая, по их мнению, необоснованно приравнивала США к враждебным государствам (России, Китаю) в сфере информационного влияния. Это произошло под прямым дипломатическим давлением администрации Трампа, для которой такая характеристика была неприемлема.

**English translation**

The EU, Canada, and Lithuania have withdrawn their support for the **#Disinfo2026** conference in Vilnius (October 7–8, 2026) for several interrelated reasons:

**1. Content of the Conference Program** The organizers (EU DisinfoLab) included sessions in the program where the United States was treated as a source of disinformation and an actor of "foreign information manipulation and interference" (FIMI). In particular, the following panels caused resonance: - **"USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back"** - **"Under attack: How the counter-disinformation community is dealing with US government pressure"** - A discussion on the role of the United States in spreading disinformation in the Arctic context

**2. Pressure from the United States** According to investigations by **Politico** and **The Guardian**, the Trump administration exerted direct diplomatic pressure on the EU, Canada, and Lithuania. The U.S. State Department sent cables to embassies asking them to express Washington's concerns and "assess" whether governments agreed with characterizing the U.S. as a FIMI actor.

**3. Official Statements from the Countries** Although the authorities deny directly submitting to U.S. pressure, they cite the inconsistency of the program with their positions:

**Lithuania**: The Ministry of Foreign Affairs stated that "parts of the program do not correspond to the government's positions and national interests." Foreign Minister **Kęstutis Budrys** said he was "surprised and shocked" that the U.S. was put on the same level as Russia or China, and that such a framing "weakens ties with allies."

**Canada**: Global Affairs Canada stated that it "reconsidered its participation" following changes to the panel formulations. Department spokeswoman Samantha Lafleur noted that Canada "is not comfortable" with such a presentation of the topic.

**EU** (European External Action Service — EEAS): Stated that the organizers formulated the discussions "in a way that does not align with the EU's official positions."

**4. Practical Consequences** - Canada and Lithuania demanded the removal of their ministries' logos from the conference materials (the logos were covered with white stickers). - The EEAS followed their example and was removed from the official program. - **France** remained the only major sponsor. - Some Canadian officials continued to participate in the conference, but without sponsor status.

Thus, the withdrawal of support occurred due to a combination of **U.S. diplomatic pressure** and **governments' disagreement with the presentation of the topic (which they perceived as anti-American)**, where the U.S. was equated with hostile states in the context of disinformation.

ЕС, Канада и Литва отозвали поддержку конференции **#Disinfo2026** в Вильнюсе (7–8 октября 2026 года) по нескольким взаимосвязанным причинам:

**1. Содержание программы конференции** Организаторы (EU DisinfoLab) включили в программу сессии, где США рассматривались как источник дезинформации и актор «иностранного информационного манипулирования и вмешательства» (FIMI). В частности, вызвали резонанс панели: - **«USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back»** («США как угроза FIMI: признать, адаптироваться, дать отпор») - **«Under attack: How the counter-disinformation community is dealing with US government pressure»** («Под атакой: как сообщество по борьбе с дезинформацией справляется с давлением правительства США») - Обсуждение роли США в распространении дезинформации в арктическом контексте

**2. Давление со стороны США** Согласно расследованию **Politico** и **The Guardian**, администрация президента Трампа оказывала прямое дипломатическое давление на ЕС, Канаду и Литву. Госдепартамент США направил телеграммы посольствам с просьбой выразить обеспокоенность Вашингтона и «оценить», согласны ли правительства с характеристикой США как FIMI-актора.

**3. Официальные заявления стран** Хотя власти отрицают прямое подчинение давлению США, они ссылаются на несоответствие программы своим позициям:

**Литва**: МИД заявил, что «части программы не соответствуют позициям правительства и национальным интересам». Министр иностранных дел **Кястутис Будрис** сказал, что был «удивлён и шокирован» тем, что США ставят в один ряд с Россией или Китаем, и что такая подача «ослабляет связи с союзниками».

**Канада**: Министерство глобальных дел Канады заявило, что «пересмотрело участие» после изменений в формулировке панелей. Представительница ведомства Саманта Лафлер отметила, что Канада «не комфортно себя чувствует» с такой подачей темы.

**ЕС** (Европейская служба внешних связей — EEAS): Заявила, что организаторы сформулировали дискуссии «таким образом, который не соответствует официальным позициям ЕС».

**4. Практические последствия** - Канада и Литва потребовали убрать логотипы своих министерств из материалов конференции (логотипы были заклеены белыми стикерами). - EEAS последовал их примеру и был исключён из официальной программы. - Единственным крупным спонсором осталась **Франция**. - Некоторые канадские чиновники продолжили участие в конференции, но без статуса спонсоров.

Таким образом, отзыв поддержки произошёл из-за сочетания **дипломатического давления США** и **несогласия правительств с антисемитской (в их понимании) подачей темы**, где США приравнивались к враждебным государствам в контексте дезинформации.

**The immediate issue was a conference panel treating the United States as a potential disinformation threat to Europe. The session examined the United States as a possible information threat and how Europe could respond. The U.S. State Department said that labeling the U.S. this way undermined shared goals. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

([techpolicy.press](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/)) - **Canada:** Global Affairs Canada cited changes to the framing of several panels and revised its participation in those sessions. Canadian officials nevertheless continued attending the conference. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719)) - **Lithuania:** Its foreign ministry said it canceled participation because parts of the programme differed from the government’s official position. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

**What “withdrawal” actually meant** It was **not a uniform, complete cutoff**. EU and Canadian representatives remained involved, and the EU reportedly continued funding the conference despite removing its official-supporter branding. ([techpolicy.press](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/))

*Sources returned by search:* - [US lobbies allies to cut ties to anti-disinformation conference over panel critical of Washington, sources say By Reuters](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719) - [„Politico“: dėl JAV spaudimo Lietuva, ES ir Kanada nerems dezinformacijos konferencijos - LRT](https://archyvai.lrt.lt/naujienos/lietuvoje/2/3076085/politico-del-jav-spaudimo-lietuva-es-ir-kanada-nerems-dezinformacijos-konferencijos) - [ЕС, Канада и Литва отозвали поддержку конференции по дезинформации под давлением США | Европейская правда](https://www.eurointegration.com.ua/rus/news/2026/10/8/7247190/) - [Disinformation Conference in Europe Caught Up in Transatlantic Politics | TechPolicy.Press](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/) - [EU drops sponsorship of disinformation conference at Trump officials’ request | Trump administration | The Guardian](https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/oct/07/trump-eu-disinformation-conference)

**English translation**

**In short: officially — due to disagreement with the program's wording; according to media reports — following diplomatic pressure from the Trump administration, which was outraged by the discussion of the US as a source of disinformation and interference in Europe.** Reuters confirmed pressure on Canada and Литва [Lithuania] based on three US sources and reviewed correspondence; other publications also reported on pressure exerted on the EU diplomatic service. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

**What caused the conflict**

The program of the conference in Vilnius on **October 7–8, 2026**, included discussions: - on the US as a threat of **FIMI** — foreign information manipulation and interference; - on countering pressure from the US government against the community of disinformation researchers, including the threat of visa bans. ([archyvai.lrt.lt](https://archyvai.lrt.lt/naujienos/lietuvoje/2/3076085/politico-del-jav-spaudimo-lietuva-es-ir-kanada-nerems-dezinformacijos-konferencijos))

Washington considered this framing of the issue unacceptable. According to a diplomatic cable described by Politico, the State Department objected to the US effectively being put on a par with Russia and China. US embassies were instructed to convey these grievances to the governments of partner countries. The State Department itself, without disclosing the content of the negotiations, stated that designating the US as an information threat undermines common goals. ([eurointegration.com.ua](https://www.eurointegration.com.ua/rus/news/2026/10/8/7247190/))

**How the participants themselves explained the decision**

- **Lithuania:** certain parts of the program diverge from the government's official position; the Ministry of Foreign Affairs canceled its participation. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

- **Canada:** changed its participation in some panels due to changes in their wording, but its officials continued to attend the conference. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

- **The EU — more precisely, the European External Action Service (EEAS):** demanded that its logo be removed, since the presentation of a number of discussions does not align with the EU's official positions. At the same time, representatives of the service remained involved in the event. ([techpolicy.press](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/))

**Therefore, "withdrew support" does not mean a complete boycott or the termination of all funding.** According to The Guardian, the EU continued to fund the conference despite the removal of its branding. France maintained its support. ([theguardian.com](https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/oct/07/trump-eu-disinformation-conference))

In other words, the dispute was not about the necessity of combating disinformation in general, but about whether it is permissible to discuss **the American ally itself as a source of information threat**. The official explanation is disagreement with the program; journalistic investigations point to Washington's pressure as the direct factor behind the decisions. ([techpolicy.press](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/))

**Original text**

**Коротко: официально — из-за несогласия с формулировками программы; по сообщениям СМИ — после дипломатического давления администрации Трампа, возмущённой обсуждением США как источника дезинформации и вмешательства в Европе.** Reuters подтвердило давление на Канаду и Литву по трём американским источникам и изученной переписке; о давлении также на дипломатическую службу ЕС сообщали другие издания. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

**Что вызвало конфликт**

В программу конференции в Вильнюсе **7–8 октября 2026 года** включили дискуссии: - о США как угрозе **FIMI** — иностранного манипулирования информацией и вмешательства; - о противодействии сообщества исследователей дезинформации давлению правительства США, включая угрозу визовых запретов. ([archyvai.lrt.lt](https://archyvai.lrt.lt/naujienos/lietuvoje/2/3076085/politico-del-jav-spaudimo-lietuva-es-ir-kanada-nerems-dezinformacijos-konferencijos))

Вашингтон счёл такую постановку вопроса неприемлемой. По дипломатической телеграмме, описанной Politico, Госдепартамент возражал против того, что США фактически ставят в один ряд с Россией и Китаем. Американским посольствам поручили донести эти претензии до правительств стран-партнёров. Сам Госдепартамент, не раскрывая содержание переговоров, заявил, что обозначение США как информационной угрозы подрывает общие цели. ([eurointegration.com.ua](https://www.eurointegration.com.ua/rus/news/2026/10/8/7247190/))

**Как объяснили решение сами участники**

- **Литва:** отдельные части программы расходятся с официальной позицией правительства; МИД отменил своё участие. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

- **Канада:** изменила участие в некоторых панелях из-за изменения их формулировок, но её чиновники продолжили посещать конференцию. ([investing.com](https://www.investing.com/news/world-news/us-lobbies-allies-to-cut-ties-to-antidisinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-of-washington-sources-say-4937719))

- **ЕС — точнее, Европейская служба внешних связей (EEAS):** потребовала убрать свой логотип, поскольку подача ряда дискуссий не соответствует официальным позициям ЕС. При этом представители службы оставались вовлечены в мероприятие. ([techpolicy.press](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/))

**Поэтому «отозвали поддержку» не означает полного бойкота или прекращения всего финансирования.** ЕС, по данным The Guardian, продолжил финансировать конференцию, несмотря на снятие своего брендинга. Франция поддержку сохранила. ([theguardian.com](https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/oct/07/trump-eu-disinformation-conference))

Иными словами, спор был не о необходимости бороться с дезинформацией вообще, а о том, допустимо ли обсуждать **самого американского союзника как источник информационной угрозы**. Официальное объяснение — несогласие с программой; журналистские расследования указывают на давление Вашингтона как непосредственный фактор решений. ([techpolicy.press](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/))

#### English — answer round 1

*Web access: Used — OpenRouter search*

*Sources returned by search:* - [EU, Canada and Lithuania pull out of disinformation ...](https://www.politico.com/news/2026/10/07/disinformation-conference-europe-us-pressure-01111134) - [Lithuania, EU and Canada withdraw support for Vilnius disinfo conference under US pressure - LRT](https://www.lrt.lt/en/news-in-english/19/3076091/lithuania-eu-and-canada-withdraw-support-for-vilnius-disinfo-conference-under-us-pressure) - [Canada yanks sponsorship of disinformation conference discussing U.S. as a threat | CBC News](https://www.cbc.ca/news/politics/gac-disinformation-united-states-lithuania-9.7187116) - [Canada drops sponsorship of conference that talks of U.S. as foreign interference actor - The Globe and Mail](https://www.theglobeandmail.com/politics/article-canada-drops-sponsorship-of-conference-that-talks-of-us-as-foreign/) - [Pressured by US, Brussels pulls out of Disinfo 2026 ...](https://www.intelligenceonline.com/europe-russia/2026/10/07/pressured-by-us-brussels-pulls-out-of-disinfo-2026-conference-in-vilnius,110892477-art)

**English translation**

**Why the EU, Canada, and Lithuania Withdrew from #Disinfo2026**

The EU, Canada, and Lithuania withdrew their support for the **#Disinfo2026** conference in Vilnius (organized by **EU DisinfoLab**) in October 2026 due to **pressure from the Trump administration** after the conference program included panels characterizing the **United States as a source of foreign disinformation**.

**What triggered the withdrawals**

The conference program featured several panels that described the U.S. as a **"foreign information manipulation and interference" (FIMI)** actor — a term typically reserved for state-backed deceptive operations to sway public debate and undermine institutions. Key panels included:

- **"USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back"** — examining how the U.S. had become a disinformation threat to Europe, featuring American researchers and a former CIA official.

- **"Under attack: How the counter-disinformation community is dealing with US government pressure"** — discussing U.S. government entry bans targeting European disinformation researchers.

- A session on the U.S. operating as a FIMI actor in **Arctic contexts**.

**How the pressure was applied**

According to excerpts of a **State Department cable** seen by Politico, the U.S. State Department directed American embassies in several European countries and the EU to:

1. **Express Washington's concerns** about the conference's framing of the U.S.

1. **"Assess"** whether the governments involved agreed with characterizing the United States as a FIMI actor.

Additionally, reporting from *Intelligence Online* indicated that an **investigation by a prominent MAGA blogger** and subsequent **social media attacks** on the conference contributed to the partner withdrawals.

**The withdrawals**

- **Canada and Lithuania** asked organizers on **Monday** to remove their ministries' logos from conference materials.

- The **European External Action Service (EEAS)**, the EU's diplomatic arm, followed on **Tuesday**.

- Photographs showed sponsor logos **covered with white stickers** on conference displays.

- **France** remained the only major sponsor listed.

**Official explanations**

The withdrawing parties gave more diplomatic public explanations:

- **Lithuania's Foreign Ministry** and the **EEAS** said they withdrew because parts of the conference program **"did not correspond with their official positions,"** without specifying which parts.

- **Canada's Global Affairs** said it was "not comfortable with the framing of some of the panels" and revised its participation — Canadian officials still attended but did not chair any discussions.

The **U.S. State Department** said it "could not discuss diplomatic conversations" but added that it "should not be a surprise that countries would disaffiliate from this conference upon learning that it labeled the U.S. a 'FIMI threat.'"

**English translation**

**The withdrawal of the EU, Canada, and Lithuania from the #Disinfo2026 conference**

This withdrawal, which occurred on **October 7-8, 2026** in Vilnius, is the result of **diplomatic pressure exerted by the Trump administration**, combined with a disagreement over the content of the program. Here are the key elements:

**📌 The direct cause: panels critical of the United States**

The conference, organized by the NGO **EU DisinfoLab**, included panel discussions examining the United States as a source of **foreign information manipulation and interference (FIMI)**. One of them was entitled:

> *« USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back »* > (The United States as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back)

Another session addressed the pressure exerted by the U.S. government on the disinformation-fighting community, notably entry bans targeting European figures.

**🇺🇸 Pressure from the U.S. Department of State**

According to Politico and Reuters, the **Department of State** actively pressured its allies: - Diplomatic cables show that Washington asked its embassies in Europe and at the EU to express its concerns. - The U.S. Ambassador to Lithuania, **Kara McDonald**, personally participated in the démarche with Vilnius. - Canada, Lithuania, and France were contacted.

A State Department spokesperson stated that it *"should not be surprising that countries are disassociating themselves from this conference after learning that it characterized the United States as a 'FIMI threat'"*.

**📋 Official justifications from the withdrawing countries**

Each entity invoked reasons related to the **content of the program**:

| Actor | Official justification |
|---|---|
| **Canada** (Global Affairs) | "Changes in the wording of several panel discussions" → participation reviewed |
| **EU** (EEAS) | "Certain discussions do not align with the EU's official positions" |
| **Lithuania** (Ministry of Foreign Affairs) | "Several parts of the program differ from the government's official position" |

**🇫🇷 The case of France**

**France remained the only major sponsor** to stay. A French diplomat indicated that his government, without having participated in the drafting of the program, regularly supported civil society efforts against disinformation.

**In summary**

This withdrawal illustrates the **growing tension** between the Trump administration and its European allies on the issue of disinformation, with Washington refusing to be characterized as an actor in information manipulation, while European researchers considered this question legitimate within the framework of a scientific conference on the subject.

**Original text**

**Le retrait de l'UE, du Canada et de la Lituanie de la conférence #Disinfo2026**

Ce retrait, survenu les **7-8 octobre 2026** à Vilnius, est le résultat d'une **pression diplomatique exercée par l'administration Trump**, combinée à un désaccord sur le contenu du programme. Voici les éléments clés :

**📌 La cause directe : des panels critiques envers les États-Unis**

La conférence, organisée par l'ONG **EU DisinfoLab**, comprenait des tables rondes examinant les États-Unis comme une source de **manipulation et ingérence étrangères dans l'information (FIMI)**. L'une d'elles était intitulée :

> *« USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back »* > (Les États-Unis en tant que menace FIMI : reconnaître, s'adapter, riposter)

Une autre session abordait les pressions exercées par le gouvernement américain sur la communauté de lutte contre la désinformation, notamment les interdits d'entrée visant des personnalités européennes.

**🇺🇸 La pression du Département d'État américain**

Selon Politico et Reuters, le **Département d'État** a activement fait pression sur ses alliés : - Des câbles diplomatiques montrent que Washington a demandé à ses ambassades en Europe et auprès de l'UE d'exprimer ses préoccupations. - L'ambassadrice des États-Unis en Lituanie, **Kara McDonald**, a personnellement participé aux démarches auprès de Vilnius. - Le Canada, la Lituanie et la France ont été contactés.

Le porte-parole du Département d'État a déclaré qu'il *« ne devrait pas être surprenant que des pays se désassocient de cette conférence après avoir appris qu'elle qualifiait les États-Unis de 'menace FIMI' »*.

**📋 Les justifications officielles des pays retirants**

Chaque entité a invoqué des raisons liées au **contenu du programme** :

| Acteur | Justification officielle |
|---|---|
| **Canada** (Affaires mondiales) | « Changements dans la formulation de plusieurs tables rondes » → participation révisée |
| **UE** (SEAE) | « Certaines discussions ne correspondent pas aux positions officielles de l'UE » |
| **Lituanie** (Ministère des AE) | « Plusieurs parties du programme diffèrent de la position officielle du gouvernement » |

**🇫🇷 Le cas de la France**

La **France est restée le seul grand sponsor** maintenu. Un diplomate français a indiqué que son gouvernement, sans avoir participé à l'élaboration du programme, soutenait régulièrement les efforts de la société civile contre la désinformation.

**En résumé**

Ce retrait illustre la **tension croissante** entre l'administration Trump et ses alliés européens sur la question de la désinformation, Washington refusant d'être caractérisé comme un acteur de manipulation de l'information, tandis que les chercheurs européens considéraient cette question comme légitime dans le cadre d'une conférence scientifique sur le sujet.

#### Lithuanian — answer round 1

*Web access: Used — OpenRouter search*

*Sources returned by search:* - [EU, Canada and Lithuania pull out of disinformation ...](https://www.politico.com/news/2026/10/07/disinformation-conference-europe-us-pressure-01111134) - [Lithuania, EU and Canada withdraw support for Vilnius ...](https://www.lrt.lt/en/news-in-english/19/3076091/lithuania-eu-and-canada-withdraw-support-for-vilnius-disinfo-conference-under-us-pressure) - [„Politico“: dėl JAV spaudimo Lietuva, ES ir Kanada nerems dezinformacijos konferencijos - LRT](https://www.lrt.lt/naujienos/lietuvoje/2/3076085/politico-del-jav-spaudimo-lietuva-es-ir-kanada-nerems-dezinformacijos-konferencijos) - [US pressure strips sponsors from a disinformation conference](https://theclarity.today/story/us-pressure-strips-sponsors-from-a-disinformation-conference-8830d31e) - [Disinformation Conference in Europe Caught Up ...](https://www.techpolicy.press/disinformation-conference-in-europe-roiled-by-transatlantic-politics/)

**English translation**

The EU, Canada, and Lithuania withdrew their support (more precisely – officially distanced themselves and asked to remove their logos from) for the international disinformation conference **"#Disinfo2026"**, held in Vilnius in early October 2026, for several main reasons:

**1. Designation of the US as a disinformation threat (FIMI)** The main reason was the conference program. The event organizers (the non-governmental organization "EU DisinfoLab") included sessions in the program where the United States was examined as an actor of "foreign information manipulation and interference" (English *FIMI*). One of the discussions was even titled *"The US as a FIMI threat: recognizing, adapting, pushing back"*. In this way, an attempt was made in the context of the conference to equate the US with Russia or China.

**2. Direct pressure from the US (Trump administration)** As reported by the media (e.g., "Politico"), the US State Department reacted very strongly and exerted direct diplomatic pressure on EU, Canadian, and Lithuanian representatives. US embassies were instructed to contact these states, express Washington's dissatisfaction, and clarify whether they agreed with the designation of the US as a disinformation threat.

**3. Incompatibility with official positions and national interests** Faced with this situation, all three parties decided to distance themselves from the event so as not to be identified with the anti-American narrative: * **Lithuania:** Lithuanian Minister of Foreign Affairs Kęstutis Budrys publicly stated that the organizers "overstepped significantly and made a major transgression" by equating the US (an ally) with Russia or China. The Lithuanian MFA indicated that parts of the program do not correspond to the government's position and Lithuania's national interests, and therefore asked to remove the ministry's logos from the event materials. * **Canada:** Global Affairs Canada also asked the organizers to remove its logos from the conference paraphernalia, citing the wording of certain discussions, although Canadian representatives continued to participate in the event. * **European Union:** The EU External Action Service (EEAS) asked to remove its logos, explaining that the organizers formulated some discussions in such a way that they do not correspond to the official EU position (in official reports, the EU attributes the FIMI threat to Russia and China, but not the US). Nevertheless, it is reported that the EU continued to fund the event, and some EEAS representatives secretly participated in the discussions.

**In summary:** The withdrawal of support (covering or removing logos) occurred because the event organizers included elements in the program that assigned the US the role of a hostile disinformation spreader. This provoked a sharp US reaction and forced allies to urgently distance themselves from such a conference agenda in order to avoid diplomatic friction and the weakening of transatlantic ties.

**Original text**

ES, Kanada ir Lietuva atsiėmė (tiksliau – oficialiai atsiribojo ir paprašė nuimti savo logotipus iš) tarptautinės dezinformacijos konferencijos **„#Disinfo2026“**, vykusios Vilniuje 2026 m. spalio pradžioje, paramą dėl kelių pagrindinių priežasčių:

**1. JAV įvardijimas kaip dezinformacijos grėsmės (FIMI)** Pagrindinė priežastis – konferencijos programa. Renginio organizatoriai (nevyriausybinė organizacija „EU DisinfoLab“) į programą įtraukė sesijas, kuriose Jungtinės Valstijos buvo nagrinėjamos kaip „užsienio informacijos manipuliacijos ir kišimosi“ (angl. *FIMI*) aktorės. Viena iš diskusijų net vadinosi *„JAV kaip FIMI grėsmė: pripažinti, prisitaikyti, stumti atgal“*. Tokiu būdu JAV konferencijos kontekste buvo bandoma lyginti su Rusija ar Kinija.

**2. Tiesioginis JAV (Trampo administracijos) spaudimas** Kaip pranešė žiniasklaida (pvz., „Politico“), JAV valstybės departamentas sureagavo labai griežtai ir padarė tiesioginį diplomatinį spaudimą ES, Kanados ir Lietuvos atstovams. JAV ambasados buvo įpareigotos susisiekti su šiomis valstybėmis, išreikšti Vašingtono nepasitenkinimą ir išsiaiškinti, ar jos pritaria JAV įvardijimui kaip dezinformacijos grėsmės.

**3. Oficialių pozicijų ir nacionalinių interesų neatitikimas** Susidūrusios su šia situacija, visos trys pusės nusprendė atsiriboti nuo renginio, kad nebūtų tapatinamos su antiamerikietišku naratyvu: * **Lietuva:** Lietuvos užsienio reikalų ministras Kęstutis Budrys viešai pareiškė, kad organizatoriai „gerokai perspaudė ir padarė didelį nusižengimą“, prilygindami JAV (sąjungininkę) Rusijai ar Kinijai. Lietuvos URM nurodė, kad programos dalys neatitinka vyriausybės pozicijos ir Lietuvos nacionalinių interesų, todėl paprašė pašalinti ministerijos logotipus iš renginio medžiagos. * **Kanada:** Kanados pasaulinių reikalų ministerija taip pat paprašė organizatorių nuimti savo logotipus nuo konferencijos atributikos, motyvuodama kai kurių diskusijų formuluotėmis, nors Kanados atstovai toliau dalyvavo renginyje. * **Europos Sąjunga:** ES Išorės veiksmų tarnyba (EIVT) paprašė pašalinti savo logotipus, paaiškindama, kad organizatoriai kai kurias diskusijas suformulavo taip, jog jos neatitinka oficialios ES pozicijos (ES oficialiuose raportuose FIMI grėsmei priskiria Rusiją ir Kiniją, bet ne JAV). Visgi, skelbiama, kad ES toliau finansavo renginį, o kai kurie EIVT atstovai slaptai dalyvavo diskusijose.

**Apibendrinant:** Paramos atsiėmimas (logotipų uždengimas ar nuėmimas) įvyko todėl, kad renginio organizatoriai įtraukė į programą elementus, kurie JAV priskyrė priešiško dezinformacijos skleidėjo vaidmenį. Tai iššaukė aštrią JAV reakciją ir privertė sąjungininkus skubiai atsiriboti nuo tokios konferencijos darbotvarkės, siekiant išvengti diplomatinių trintų ir transatlantinių ryšių silpninimo.

**English translation**

**Why the EU, Canada, and Lithuania withdrew support for the #Disinfo2026 conference**

The main reason is **pressure from the Trump administration** after the conference organizers included panel discussions in the program that **characterized the United States as an actor of "Foreign Information Manipulation and Interference" (FIMI)**.

**Chronology of events**

**The conference program** (#Disinfo2026, organized by the Brussels-based NGO EU DisinfoLab) included sessions dedicated to the role of the US as a source of disinformation. One of the panels was titled *"USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back"* and focused on how the US has become a disinformation threat to Europe, including in the Arctic context.

**The US State Department** sent a diplomatic cable to US embassies in several European countries and the EU demanding that Washington's concerns be expressed and that it be "assessed" whether the governments of these countries agree with the characterization of the US as a FIMI actor. According to Reuters, US Ambassador to Lithuania Kara McDonald, in particular, was involved in this process.

**Under US pressure**: - **Canada** and **Lithuania** asked the organizers on Monday to remove their ministries' logos from the conference materials. - **The European External Action Service (EEAS)** — the EU's diplomatic service — followed suit on Tuesday.

In photographs from the conference, the logos of Canada, Lithuania, and the EU were **covered with white stickers**. The **French** government became the only major sponsor remaining on the list.

**Official positions**

- **The US State Department** stated that "it should come as no surprise that countries distanced themselves from the conference upon learning that it labels the US a 'FIMI threat'."

- **Canada** (Global Affairs Canada) stated that it had reviewed its participation due to "changes in the phrasing of certain panel discussions," but did not deny that it had objections to describing the US as a threat.

- **Lithuania** — the Ministry of Foreign Affairs stated that "certain parts of the program do not align with the government's positions and Lithuania's national interests." Lithuanian Foreign Minister Kęstutis Budrys said he was "surprised and shocked" that the US was being put on a par with Russia or China, calling it an "anti-American narrative that weakens ties with allies."

- **The EEAS** (EU) stated that the organizers "chose a format for certain discussions that does not align with the EU's official positions."

**Context**

According to *The Intelligence Online*, the trigger was also an investigation by an influential MAGA blogger and subsequent social media attacks on the conference, which increased political pressure on the participants.

**Original text**

**Почему ЕС, Канада и Литва отозвали поддержку конференции #Disinfo2026**

Основная причина — **давление со стороны администрации Трампа** после того, как организаторы конференции включили в программу панельные дискуссии, в которых **США характеризовались как субъект «иностранной манипуляции информацией и вмешательства» (FIMI — Foreign Information Manipulation and Interference)**.

**Хронология событий**

**Программа конференции** (#Disinfo2026, организованной брюссельской НКО EU DisinfoLab) включала сессии, посвящённые роли США как источника дезинформации. Одна из панелей называлась *«USA as a FIMI threat: acknowledge, adapt, push back»* и была посвящена тому, как США стали угрозой дезинформации для Европы, в том числе в арктическом контексте.

**Госдепартамент США** разослал дипломатическую телеграмму в посольства США в ряде европейских стран и в ЕС с требованием выразить обеспокоенность Вашингтона и «оценить», согласны ли правительства этих стран с характеристикой США как субъекта FIMI. По данным Reuters, к этому процессу была причастна, в частности, посол США в Литве Кара Макдональд.

**Под давлением** США: - **Канада** и **Литва** в понедельник попросили организаторов убрать логотипы их министерств с материалов конференции. - **Европейская служба внешних действий (EEAS)** — дипломатическая служба ЕС — последовала их примеру во вторник.

На фотографиях с конференции логотипы Канады, Литвы и ЕС были **заклеены белыми наклейками**. Единственным крупным спонсором, оставшимся в списке, стало правительство **Франции**.

**Официальные позиции**

- **Госдепартамент США** заявил, что «не должно быть сюрпризом, что страны дистанцировались от конференции, узнав, что она называет США "угрозой FIMI"».

- **Канада** (Global Affairs Canada) заявила, что пересмотрела своё участие из-за «изменений в формулировках некоторых панельных дискуссий», но не стала отрицать, что у неё были возражения против описания США как угрозы.

- **Литва** — МИД заявил, что «некоторые части программы не соответствуют позициям правительства и национальным интересам Литвы». Глава МИД Литвы Кястутис Будрис сказал, что был «удивлён и шокирован» тем, что США ставят в один ряд с Россией или Китаем, и назвал это «антиамериканским нарративом, ослабляющим связи с союзниками».

- **EEAS** (ЕС) заявила, что организаторы «выбрали такой формат некоторых дискуссий, который не соответствует официальным позициям ЕС».

**Контекст**

По данным издания *The Intelligence Online*, толчком также стало расследование влиятельного блогера MAGA и последовавшие за ним атаки в социальных сетях на конференцию, что усилило политическое давление на участников.

**English translation**

They withdrew official sponsorship because parts of the program treated the United States as a disinformation threat, after US diplomatic pressure over that framing.

The #Disinfo2026 conference, run by the Brussels nonprofit EU DisinfoLab in Vilnius on 7–8 October 2026, included sessions that described the US as a “foreign information manipulation and interference” (FIMI) actor. One panel was titled along the lines of “USA as a FIMI threat: adapt, acknowledge, push back” and asked how the United States had become a FIMI threat for Europe. Another addressed US government pressure on the counter-disinformation community. The program had been public for weeks or months beforehand.

Reporting by *The Guardian*, *Politico*, and Reuters says the Trump administration’s State Department lobbied Lithuania, Canada, and others (including France) to pull back. A State Department cable and internal documents described the US-as-FIMI framing as a false equivalence with adversaries such as Russia and China that “undermines shared goals.” US officials reportedly thanked Lithuania for taking the concerns seriously; Lithuania’s foreign minister is said to have told the US embassy that the US-focused sessions did not represent the Lithuanian government’s views. France stayed on as a main supporter. Logos of the EU’s External Action Service (EEAS), Global Affairs Canada, and Lithuania’s foreign ministry were removed or covered on materials shortly before the event.

Their public explanations were narrower and did not confirm US pressure:

- **Lithuania** said it cancelled participation because several parts of the programme differed from the government’s official position and national interests, and asked organizers not to link the ministry to the event.

- **Canada** said that after changes to the framing of several panels it revised its participation in those sessions and withdrew sponsorship, while Canadian officials still attended.

- **The EU (EEAS)** said organizers framed some discussions in a way that did not align with official EU positions. EU officials did not accept characterizing the US as a disinformation actor; the EEAS’s own FIMI reporting has focused on actors such as Russia and China. The EU reportedly continued to fund the conference even after dropping its branding as a supporter.

The US State Department said it should not be surprising that countries would disaffiliate once the US was labeled a “FIMI threat,” a term normally used for adversary influence operations.

**Original text**

They withdrew official sponsorship because parts of the program treated the United States as a disinformation threat, after US diplomatic pressure over that framing.

The #Disinfo2026 conference, run by the Brussels nonprofit EU DisinfoLab in Vilnius on 7–8 October 2026, included sessions that described the US as a “foreign information manipulation and interference” (FIMI) actor. One panel was titled along the lines of “USA as a FIMI threat: adapt, acknowledge, push back” and asked how the United States had become a FIMI threat for Europe. Another addressed US government pressure on the counter-disinformation community. The program had been public for weeks or months beforehand.

Reporting by *The Guardian*, *Politico*, and Reuters says the Trump administration’s State Department lobbied Lithuania, Canada, and others (including France) to pull back. A State Department cable and internal documents described the US-as-FIMI framing as a false equivalence with adversaries such as Russia and China that “undermines shared goals.” US officials reportedly thanked Lithuania for taking the concerns seriously; Lithuania’s foreign minister is said to have told the US embassy that the US-focused sessions did not represent the Lithuanian government’s views. France stayed on as a main supporter. Logos of the EU’s External Action Service (EEAS), Global Affairs Canada, and Lithuania’s foreign ministry were removed or covered on materials shortly before the event.

Their public explanations were narrower and did not confirm US pressure:

- **Lithuania** said it cancelled participation because several parts of the programme differed from the government’s official position and national interests, and asked organizers not to link the ministry to the event.

- **Canada** said that after changes to the framing of several panels it revised its participation in those sessions and withdrew sponsorship, while Canadian officials still attended.

- **The EU (EEAS)** said organizers framed some discussions in a way that did not align with official EU positions. EU officials did not accept characterizing the US as a disinformation actor; the EEAS’s own FIMI reporting has focused on actors such as Russia and China. The EU reportedly continued to fund the conference even after dropping its branding as a supporter.

The US State Department said it should not be surprising that countries would disaffiliate once the US was labeled a “FIMI threat,” a term normally used for adversary influence operations.

**English translation**

The EU (via the European External Action Service), Canada (Global Affairs Canada) and Lithuania withdrew their official support for the #Disinfo2026 conference, organized by EU DisinfoLab in Vilnius on October 7 and 8, 2026, because certain sessions in the program did not correspond to their official positions.

The main point of friction was panels that presented the United States as an actor of "foreign information manipulation and interference" (FIMI), notably a session entitled "USA as a FIMI threat: adapt, acknowledge, push back." This wording, which places the United States in a category usually reserved for actors like Russia or China, was deemed incompatible with the positions of these governments.

**Official positions:** - Lithuania canceled its participation, considering that several parts of the program diverged from the government's position. - Canada revised its participation after changes to the framing of several panels (though Canadian officials remained present at the conference). - The EEAS withdrew its support because the organizers had formulated certain discussions in a way not aligned with the EU's official positions.

According to reports by *The Guardian*, *Politico*, and *Reuters*, this withdrawal came after diplomatic demarches by the Trump administration and the U.S. State Department. Washington contested this characterization of the United States, describing it as a false equivalence with adversaries and as harmful to shared objectives, and contacted several capitals (including Vilnius, Ottawa, and Paris). France remained the primary visible supporter. The logos of the EU, Canada, and Lithuania were removed from the website and covered up on conference materials shortly before its opening.

**Original text**

L’UE (via le Service européen pour l’action extérieure), le Canada (Affaires mondiales Canada) et la Lituanie ont retiré leur soutien officiel à la conférence #Disinfo2026, organisée par EU DisinfoLab à Vilnius les 7 et 8 octobre 2026, parce que certaines sessions du programme ne correspondaient pas à leurs positions officielles.

Le point de friction principal était des panels qui présentaient les États-Unis comme un acteur de « foreign information manipulation and interference » (FIMI), notamment une session intitulée « USA as a FIMI threat: adapt, acknowledge, push back ». Cette formulation, qui place les États-Unis dans une catégorie habituellement réservée à des acteurs comme la Russie ou la Chine, a été jugée incompatible avec les positions de ces gouvernements.

**Positions officielles :** - La Lituanie a annulé sa participation, estimant que plusieurs parties du programme divergeaient de la position du gouvernement. - Le Canada a révisé sa participation après des changements dans le cadrage de plusieurs panels (des fonctionnaires canadiens restaient toutefois présents à la conférence). - L’EEAS a retiré son soutien parce que les organisateurs avaient formulé certaines discussions d’une manière non alignée avec les positions officielles de l’UE.

Selon des reportages du *Guardian*, de *Politico* et de *Reuters*, ce retrait est intervenu après des démarches diplomatiques de l’administration Trump et du Département d’État américain. Washington a contesté cette caractérisation des États-Unis, la qualifiant de fausse équivalence avec des adversaires et de nuisible aux objectifs communs, et a contacté plusieurs capitales (dont Vilnius, Ottawa et Paris). La France est restée le principal soutien affiché. Les logos de l’UE, du Canada et de la Lituanie ont été retirés du site et masqués sur les supports de la conférence peu avant son ouverture.

**English translation**

The EU, Canada, and Lithuania have withdrawn official support for the #Disinfo2026 conference in Vilnius following pressure from the US (Trump administration) over parts of the program that presented the US as an information threat to Europe.

The conference (October 7–8, 2026, organized by the Brussels-based NGO EU DisinfoLab) featured sessions in its program describing the US as a "foreign information manipulation and interference" (FIMI) actor. One of them—"USA as a FIMI threat: adapt, acknowledge, push back"—examined how Europe could strengthen its resilience without Washington; another explored how the counter-disinformation community is reacting to pressure from the US government. The State Department viewed this as a false equivalence with Russia and China and urged allies to distance themselves.

Following contacts with US diplomats: - **Lithuania** withdrew its participation and asked to remove the logo of the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, stating that several parts of the program do not align with the Government's official position and national interests. - **Canada** (Global Affairs Canada) reviewed its participation due to changed wording in several discussions; official officials still attended the conference, but the sponsor status and logo were removed. - **The EU** (European External Action Service) withdrew its support and logo because the organizers formulated some discussions in a way that did not align with the EU's official positions. EU funding for the conference reportedly continued.

The logos were covered up on both the website and in printed materials just days before the event. France remained the sole major sponsor and did not withdraw its support.

Sources: [The Guardian](https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/oct/07/trump-eu-disinformation-conference), [Politico](https://www.politico.com/news/2026/10/07/disinformation-conference-europe-us-pressure-01111134), [Reuters](https://www.reuters.com/world/americas/us-lobbies-allies-cut-ties-anti-disinformation-conference-over-panel-critical-2026-10-07/), [LRT](https://www.lrt.lt/en/news-in-english/19/3076091/lithuania-eu-and-canada-withdraw-support-for-vilnius-disinfo-conference-under-us-pressure).

ES, Kanada ir Lietuva atsiėmė oficialią paramą #Disinfo2026 konferencijai Vilniuje po JAV (Trumpo administracijos) spaudimo dėl programos dalių, kuriose JAV buvo pristatomos kaip informacinė grėsmė Europai.

Konferenciją (2026 m. spalio 7–8 d., organizavo Briuselio nevyriausybinė organizacija EU DisinfoLab) programoje buvo sesijos, kuriose JAV apibūdinamos kaip „foreign information manipulation and interference“ (FIMI) veikėjas. Viena jų – „USA as a FIMI threat: adapt, acknowledge, push back“ – nagrinėjo, kaip Europa galėtų stiprinti atsparumą be Vašingtono; kita – kaip kovos su dezinformacija bendruomenė reaguoja į JAV vyriausybės spaudimą. Valstybės departamentas tai laikė klaidinga lygybe su Rusija ir Kinija ir ragino sąjungininkus atsiriboti.

Po kontaktų su JAV diplomatais: - **Lietuva** atšaukė dalyvavimą ir paprašė pašalinti Užsienio reikalų ministerijos logotipą, teigdama, kad kelios programos dalys neatitinka Vyriausybės oficialios pozicijos ir nacionalinių interesų. - **Kanada** (Global Affairs Canada) peržiūrėjo dalyvavimą dėl pasikeitusio kelių diskusijų formulavimo; oficialūs pareigūnai konferencijoje vis tiek dalyvavo, bet rėmėjo statusas ir logotipas buvo pašalinti. - **ES** (Europos išorės veiksmų tarnyba) atsiėmė paramą ir logotipą, nes organizatoriai kai kurias diskusijas suformulavo taip, kad jos neatitiko ES oficialių pozicijų. ES finansavimas konferencijai, anot pranešimų, tęsėsi.

Logotipai buvo uždengti ir svetainėje, ir spausdintoje medžiagoje likus kelioms dienoms iki renginio. Prancūzija liko vienintele pagrindine rėmėja ir paramos neatšaukė.
